iṉi, ellāt tuṉpaṅkaḷum piṟattaṟkiṭamāṉa āṇavamalattiṉ kuṟumpukku aṭaiyāḷamāvatu puliyē yākaliṉ, ammalattiṉ ceyalai oṭukkiniṟkum iṟaivaṉatu  nilaiyē, pulittōlai yurittu uṭuttirukkum nilaiyāka vaittu uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉamē iṟaivaṉ ṟirumēṉimicai yaṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷāka moḻiyap paṭuvaṉa vellām arumperu nuṇporuḷkaḷai uḷḷaṭakkiya uruvaka vamaippukkaḷē yākum; avai yellām īṇṭeṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ itu mika viriyum. itukāṟuṅ kāṭṭiyaḷavē īṇṭaikkup pōtumākaliṉ, ivaṟṟai ittuṇaiyiṉ niṟutti mēṟcelvām.
iṟaivaṉ ṟiruvuruva aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷāka vaittu mēlē kāṭṭiyavāṟu uruvakañceytu, ellāmvalla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷai ōr uruvattirumēṉiyiṟ koṇṭu vaḻipaṭum muṟai, ettaṉaiyō āyira māṇṭukaḷukku muṟṟoṭṭē nikaḻntu varuvatoṉṟām. iṟaivaṉaic civanta niṟaṉuṭaiya `cēyōṉ ākavum, nīlaniṟaṉuṭaiya `māyōṉ ākavuṅkoṇṭu vaḻipaṭun tiruvuruva vaṇakkam.
māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟai yulakamum
cēyōṉ mēya maivarai yulakamum
eṉṉun tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārac cūttirattilēyē eṭuttuc collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. `paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭumuṉ av yāṟu pāynta perunilap parappai aracāṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ palyākacālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉpāṉ civapirāṉ tirukkōyilai valamvantu paṇiyum iyalpiṉaṉeṉpatu,
paṇiyiya rattainiṉ kuṭaiyē muṉivar
mukkaṭcelvar nakarvalañ ceyaṟkē	 (puṟanāṉūṟu, 6)
eṉṟu avaṉuṭaṉirunta kārikiḻār eṉṉum nallicaip pulavar pāṭiya pāṭṭāṉ naṉkupulaṉākiṉṟatu. ippāṇṭi maṉṉaṉ avaiyilirunta maṟṟoru nallicaip pulavarāṉa neṭṭimaiyār eṉpār ivaṉai vāḻttukiṉṟuḻi,
munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē	(puṟanāṉūṟu, 9)
eṉpataṉāṟ paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟu maṇaliṉum pala āṇṭu vāḻkaveṉa avaṉai vāḻttutal koṇṭu, mutukuṭumi eṉum ap pāṇṭivēntaṉum, avaṉatu avaikkaḷattup pulavarkaḷāṉa `kārikiḻār, `neṭṭimaiyār mutaliyōrum paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṭalkoḷḷappaṭum muṉ aḵtāvatu iṟṟaikku aiyāyim āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē iruntōrātal naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum.
iṉi, tolkāppiyam ākkappaṭṭa kālattil muttī vaḻipāṭum, am muttīkkaṇṇum muṉaittu viḷaṅkum iṟaivaṉ civanta mēṉiyaṉāyc `cēyōṉ eṉa niṟpaṉeṉṉuṅ kōṭpāṭum mikku vaḻaṅkiṉavāyiṉum, `civaṉeṉṉum peyarātalāl, `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉṉu peyarālātal iṟaivaṉai vaṇaṅkum vaṇakkam aññāṉṟu uḷatāyiṟṟillai. `mukkaṇṇaṉ `civaṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyattil ōriṭattāyiṉuṅ kāṇappaṭavillai. iṟṟaikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kārikiḻāratu ceyyuḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉṉuñ col tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭāmaiyai uṟṟu nōkkuṅkāl, tolkāppiyaṅ kārikiḻāratu kālattiṟkum paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭu iyaṟṟappaṭṭateṉpatu teḷiyap paṭum. paṭavē, tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil vaṇaṅkappaṭṭa teyvaṅkaḷ murukakkaṭavuḷum, akkaṭavuḷ muṉaittu viḷaṅkum ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ tī eṉṉum muttīyum, māyōṉumēyām.
iṉi, irukkuvētattil uruttirak kaṭavuṇmēṟpāṭap paṭṭa pākkaḷaiyum, ēṉaic ciṟuteyvaṅkaṇmēṟ pāṭappaṭṭa pākkaḷaiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkuṅkāl, ēṉaiyōraip pāṭiya pākkaḷiṉum, uruttiraṉmēṟ pāṭiyavai mikac cilavātal ārāyccivallārk kellām eḷitiṉ viḷaṅkuvatēyām. ac cilavaṟṟuḷḷum `uruttiraṉ eṉṉum peyaraṉṟi, `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉum peyar kāṇap paṭukiṉṟilatu. irukkuvētap pattu maṇṭilaṅkaḷuḷ, ēṉai oṉpatu maṇṭilaṅkaḷaiviṭak kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭatākiya pattām maṇṭilan toṇṇūṟṟiraṇṭām patikam oṉpatāñ ceyyuḷilmaṭṭum ēpi:civa eṉac `civaṉ eṉumpeyar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cukkila ecur vētattiṉ kaṇṇatāṉa `catarutriyattil uruttiraṉ, civaṉ, carvaṉ, pavaṉ, caṅkaraṉ, pacupati, īcāṉaṉ mutalāṉa civapirāṉ peyarkaḷellām oruṅkē kāṇappaṭiṉum, `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarmaṭṭum āṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟilatu. `traiyampakaṉ eṉum orucoṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum, aḵtu `ampikaiyākiya ammaiyōṭu kūṭiṉavaṉ eṉap poruḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa tallāmal, mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉap poruḷ ceyyappaṭu kiṉṟilatu. irukku, ecur mutalāṉa vētaṅkaḷ eṅkaṇum `āyiraṅkaṇkaḷuṭaiyavaṉ civapirāṉ eṉpatu collap paṭukiṉṟatēyallāmal `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉpatu ōriṭattāyiṉuñ collappaṭavillai. paṇṭait teṉṟamiḻ makkaḷiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷaip peritun taḻuvi eḻutiya itikācaṅkaḷ, purāṇaṅkaḷ, upaniṭataṅkaḷ mutalāyiṉ īṇṭaikku mēṟkōḷkaḷ ākā. mikap paḻaiya tamiḻnūl vaṭanūlkaḷai naṉkāyntu kāṇuṅkāl, ellāmvalla iṟaivaṟku, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṉum mūṉṟu oḷikaḷaiyum mukkaṇkaḷāka amaittuc, civantoḷirum ulakuruviṉaiyē avaṟku oru tirumēṉiyākak kaṟpittu avaṉai vaṇaṅkum vaṇakkam paṇṭait tamiḻmakkaṭku maṭṭumē urittātal iṉitu viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
iṉṉum, iraṇṭāyirattu munnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻnūlkaḷ tamiḻppāṭṭukkaḷilātal vaṭanūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻip pāṭṭukkaḷilātal, kaṭavuḷai nāṉmukaṉ tirumāl civapirāṉ eṉa muttiṟappaṭuttu `mummūrtti eṉṟōti, ammūvaruṭ civapirāṉum oruvaṉ eṉṉum poruntāk kōṭpāṭu oru ciṟitāyiṉuṅ kāṇap paṭāmaiyum, avaṟṟiṉkaṇṇellāñ civapirāṉ piṟappu iṟappu illā ellām valla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākavē vaittu moḻiyappaṭutalum peritum niṉaiviṟ patikkaṟ pālaṉavākum.
iṉi, uruttirapperumāṉ civanta niṟattiṉaṉāyt tikaḻtalōṭu, ceṉṉiyiṟ cekkarccaṭaikaḷum uṭaiyaṉā yirukkiṉṟaṉa ṉeṉpatu irukkuvētattiṟ palakālum nuvalappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu. itaṟku, ataṉ mutaṉ maṇṭilattil imā :rutrāya tavasē kapartinē kṣayatvīrāyaeṉṟu toṭaṅkum 114 ām patikamē cāṉṟām. itaṉkaṭ pōnta `kapartin eṉṉuñcoṟ `caṭaiyaṉ eṉṉum poruṭṭu. ituvum, itaṉōṭotta irukkuvētap patikaṅkaḷ ēṉaiyavum nālāyirattu nāṉūṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavākum.
iṉṉuṅ, kiṟittu camayattavarkkuriya mutalākamattil `iṟaivaṉ āpirakāmukku etirē tōṉṟi aruḷ ceytakkāl taṉ peyar elcaṭai eṉa moḻintāṉ eṉpatu collap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. mēlum, `el eṉṉum aṭaimoḻiyiṉṟic `caṭai eṉṉum peyarē iṟaivaṟ kuriya iyaṟpeyarāka `jāp eṉṉum ākamattil muppattorumuṟai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.27 ic `caṭai eṉṉuñcol tamiḻccollātal aṟiyāta īpurumoḻip pulavar, itaṉ piṟappum poruḷun teriyāmaiyiṟ peritum iṭarppaṭṭu mayaṅkuvārāyiṉar. `āpirakām mutalāṉa paṇṭaināyaṉmārkaḷ tamiḻarē yeṉpatu ārāycciyiṟ pulaṉātaliṉ, avar tamakkut tōṉṟiya iṟaivaṉ taṉ peyaraic `caṭaiyaṉ eṉat tamiḻmoḻiyil aruḷic ceytatu cālap poruntuvatēyām eṉka.
iṉi, iṟṟaikku iraṇṭāyirattu munnūṟu āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ `cevvēḷ mēṟpāṭappaṭṭa `kuṉṟam pūtaṉāratu paripāṭaṟ ceyyuḷiṟ, civapirāṉ taṉ caṭaik kaṟṟaiyiṟ kaṅkai nīrait tāṅkiniṟkum iyalpu,
erimalart tāmarai iṟaivīḻtta peruvāri
viricaṭaip poṟaiyūḻttu viḻunikar malarēyppat
taṇivuṟat tāṅkiya taṉinilaic calatāri
maṇimiṭaṟ ṟaṇṇaṟku matiyāraṟ piṟantōy nī
eṉṟu teḷivākak kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka. `tirumāliṉ tiruvaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅkiya kaṅkai nīraic civapirāṉ taṉatu caṭaikkaṇ ēṟṟāṉ eṉap piṟkālattu vaiṇavar muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷai iḻitturaippāṉ vēṇṭik kaṭṭiviṭṭa poykkatai paḻaiya tamiḻnūl vaṭanūlkaḷiṟ ciṟituṅ kāṇappaṭāmaiyum īṇṭuniṉaiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu.
iṉi, iṟaivaṉ tirumuṭimēṟ piṟaiyamarntu viḷaṅkun tōṟṟam, iṟṟaikku āyiratteṇṇūṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ pāṭappaṭṭaṉavākiya.

yāḻkeḻu maṇimiṭaṟ ṟantaṇaṉ
tāvil tāḷniḻaṟ ṟavirntaṉṟāl ulakē	 (akam, 1)
eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup paḻantamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ ṟeḷitturaikkappaṭ ṭiruttal kāṇka. ēṉait tiruvuruva vaṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷum iv vakanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuṭkaṇ ōviyatteḻutik kāṭṭiṉāṟpōl attuṇai eḻilpeṟak kūṟappaṭṭiruttalum viyakkaṟpālatoṉṟām.
ivvāṟāka oḷiyoṭukūṭi viḷaṅkum ulakat tōṟṟattiṉaiyē uruvaka vakaiyāl iṟaivaṉṟaṉ tiruvuruvamāka vaittu vaḻipaṭṭa paṇṭait tamiḻc cāṉ ṟōril, antaṇar eṉpār vēṟu muyaṟciyiṟ pukutaliṉṟi, iṟaivaṟku vaḻipāṭu āṟṟutaliṉum, iṟaivaṉ nūl ōtutaliṉumē karuttu oruppaṭṭu niṉṟaṉarākaliṉ, avarellām av viṟaivaṉ ṟiruvuruvattōṭu otta tavavaṭivamē tamakkum uriyatākak koṇṭiruppārāyiṉar aracaraic cārntār perumpāṉmaiyum av varacarō ṭotta kōlattiṉaiyum, muṉivaraic cārntār perum pāṉmaiyum am muṉivarōṭotta kōlattiṉaiyum, ēṉaip palvakai nilaiyiṉ niṟpāraic cārntār perum pāṉmaiyum avvan nilaiyiṉarkkuriya kōlattiṉaiyun tān tām mēṟkoḷḷutal iyalpātalpōla, ellām valla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷaic cārntārum perumpāṉmaiyum ak kaṭavuṭkuriya tirukkōlattiṉait tāmum mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukutalum iyalpāyiṟṟeṉpatu. eṉavē, iṟaivaṉ poṉ tōṉṟa oḷivaṭiviṉaṉātal paṟṟi avaṉ vaḻiyil niṟpārāṉa aruntavattōr tāmun taḻalvaṭiviṉatākiya kāviyāṭai uṭuppārāyiṉar. taḻaltāṉ paṟṟiya poruḷait tūya cāmparākki ac cāmparait taṉmēṟ pūkka aṇintirukkum iyalpē iṟaivaṉṟaṉ tirumēṉimicait timirntirukkun tirunīṟṟiṉ uṇmaiyātal kaṇṭu, avaṉai vaḻipaṭuñ cāṉṟōrun tantirumēṉi mēlun tirunīṟu pūcāniṟpar. iṟaivaṉ taṉatu neṟṟimēṟ kaṇṇoṭu mukkaṇṇaṉāy viḷaṅkutalōṭu oppavē, cāṉṟōrun tamatu neṟṟimicaic cāntiṉāṟ poṭṭiṭṭiruppar. iṟaivaṉ taṉ tirumuṭimēṟ caṭaikkaṟṟai uṭaiyaṉāy iruttal pōlavē, aruntamiḻt tāpatarun tañceṉṉimēṟ caṭaimuṭiyuṭaiya rāyiruppar. iṟaivaṉ taṉ caṭaikkaṟṟaimicaic cumantirukkuṅ kaṅkainīrkku aṭaiyāḷamākavē, tamiḻt tāpatarun tam kaiyil taṇṇīrk kuṭuvai ēntiyiruppar. iṟaivaṉaippōl taṇṇīrait tām talaimicait tāṅkutal ākāmaiyiṉ, aruntavattōr ataṉait talaiyilaṉṟik kaiyiṉkaṭ ṭāṅkuvārāyiṉa reṉṟu uṇarntukoḷka. iṉṉum, mummalaṅkaḷaic cekukkum iṟaivaṉatu pērāṟṟalē avaṉ taṉ kaiyil ēntiniṟkum muttalai vēlākac collappaṭutaliṉ, avaṉatu tiruk kōlattait tāṅkiya tāpatarum `mukkōl ēntiyiruppar; itupaṟṟiyē ivar `mukkōṟ pakavareṉavum moḻiyappaṭuvar28. iṉṉum, āṇavamalattiṉ valiyai yoṭukkiya iṟaivaṉatu nilaiyē, avaṉ pulittōlai uṭuttiyirukkum iruppātaliṉ, avaṉvaḻic cārnta tāpatar tāmum pulittōlai mēṟkoṇṭiruppar; iṉi, `māṉ eṉṉuñcol tamiḻil māṉ eṉṉum vilaṅkiṉaiyum, vaṭamoḻiyiṟ `pirakirutimāyaiyiṉaiyuṅ kuṟippatoṉ ṟākaliṉ29 māyaiyai yoṭukkiniṟkum nilaikku aṭaiyāḷa māka māṉṟōlai irukkaiyāyk koṇṭum iruppa reṉka. iṉṉum ellā nūṟkalvikkum iṟaivaṉē talaivaṉ eṉpataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka avaṉ taṉatu valakkaiyiṟ cuvaṭiyoṉṟēnti amarntiruttal pōla, avaṉ vaḻippaṭṭa tāpatarun tamatu kaiyil nūl oṉṟēntiyiruppa reṉka. ivvāṟāka iṟaivaṉōṭotta vaṭivukoḷḷun tamiḻantaṇariṉ tavavaṭiva aṭaiyāḷaṅ kaḷiṟ cila, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉarāl,
nūlē karakam mukkōlai maṇaiyē
āyuṅ kālai antaṇarkku uriya
eṉṟu ōtappaṭṭamai kāṇka. ic cūttirattiṉkaṭ collappaṭṭa `nūl eṉpatu `kalainūlē yallāmaṟ pūṇul aṉṟu. tolkāppiyaṉār nūl eḻutiya paṇṭaikkālattiṟ purinūl pūṇum vaḻakkam uṇṭeṉpatai nāṭṭutaṟkuc cāṉṟu iṉmaiyāṉum, tuṟavoḻukkattiṉ niṟpārkkup pūṇūl iṉmai vaṭanūlārkkum uṭampāṭakalāṉum,30 itaṟkup pūṇul eṉṟu uraikūṟiya piṟkālat turaikārarāṉa `pērāciriyar kūṟṟup poruntāmai kaṇṭukoḷka. `karakam eṉpatu nīrkkuṭuvai. `mukkōl iṟaivaṉēntiya muttalai vēlukku aṭaiyāḷam. `maṇai iruttaṟku iṭum pulittōl māṉṟōl mutaliyaṉa. iṉṉun tavavoḻukkam mēṟkoṇṭa antaṇar talaimicaic caṭaimuṭiyuṭaiyarā yiruppareṉpatu,
kaṭunteṟaṟ centī vēṭṭup
puṟantāḻ puricaṭai pularttu vōṉē	(251)
eṉṉum paḻaiya puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷāl iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum. iṉṉum avar kāviyāṭai pūṇṭu mukkōl kaikkoṇṭu tavavoḻukkattiṉ niṟṟal,
kaltōyttu uṭutta paṭivap pārppāṉ,
mukkōl acainilai kaṭuppa
eṉṉum paḻaiya mullaippāṭṭu aṭikaḷāṟ pulaṉā kiṉṟatu. iṉṉum avar mēṟkūṟiya tava aṭaiyāḷaṅ kaḷōṭu, tan tirumēṉimēl tirunīṟum pūciyiruppareṉpatu,
cuttiya pokkaṇattu eṉpaṇi
kaṭṭaṅkañ cūḻcaṭai veṇ
pottiya kōlattiṉīr	(242)
eṉṟu tirucciṟṟampalak kōvaiyāril māviratiyar viḷikkappaṭumāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉamākap paṇṭaikkālattut taṇṭamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta tāpatarellārum,ellām valla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷai `murukaṉ
 `civaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl muttīkkaṇ vaṇaṅki, am muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷukkut tām uruvaka vakaiyāṟ kaṟpitta vaṭivattaiyē tāmum pūṇṭu, tavavoḻukkattiṉ kaṇṇarāy āṅkāṅku vaikiṉamaiyē paḻaiya centamiḻ nūlkaḷāṉ aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟoḻukiya aruntavattōriṉ vaṭivamum, avar tan tavavoḻukkamum, av voḻukkattiṉ pālatāṉa nōṉpum, pārppaṉariṉ tuṟavoḻukkamumē `paṭimai `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷāl uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatai mēlē 654, 656 ām pakkaṅkaḷiṟ paḻaiyanūl mēṟkōḷkaḷ pala koṇṭu naṉku viḷakkikkāṭṭiṉēm. tolkāppiyam mutalāka vanta paṇṭait tamiḻnūlkaḷil ōriṭattāṉum `paṭimai `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, camaṇmuṉivariṉ tavavoḻukkattiṉai uṇarttutaṟku mēṟkōḷ kaṇṭilam. karikāṟcōḻaṉ kālattiṟku aḵtāvatu iṟṟaikku āyirattut toḷḷāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉkaṭ camaṇcamayamātal ataṉait taḻuviṉōrātalirunta reṉpataṟku ētoru cāṉṟum paḻaiya tamiḻnūlkaḷ tamiḻppāṭṭukaḷil ōr eṭṭuṇaiyuṅ kāṇappaṭavillai.
uṇmai yivvāṟiruppat, tolkāppiyaṉāraic camaṇ muṉivareṉa nāṭṭappukkavar avar kālattiṟ camaṇmatan tamiḻnāṭṭil iruntateṉṉun tamatu kūṟṟai meyccāṉṟu oṉṟutāṉuṅ kāṭṭi niṟuvāmal avaraic camaṇareṉak kūṟappukkatu, paḻantamiḻ nūlārāycci yuṭaiyārāl nakaiyāṭi eḷḷappaṭutaṟkē iṭaṉāyatu kāṇka. mēlum avar `paṭimai eṉṉuñ collukkut `tavavoḻukkam eṉṉum poruḷ uḷatātalaik kaṇṭilēm eṉavum, tām viṉaviya vaṭamoḻip pulavar kaḷuṅkūṭa accollum poruḷum illaiyeṉṟu teḷintēm eṉavum uraippārāyiṉar.31
paḻaiya tamiḻnūlkaḷai ārāyntupārāmalē avar aṅṅaṉan tuṇivurai nikaḻttiyatu iraṅkaṟpālatoṉṟām.
kūṟiṉai perumaniṉ paṭimai yāṉē
eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattilum,
taṇṭoṭu piṭitta tāḻkamaṇ ṭalattup
  paṭiva vuṇṭip pārppaṉa makaṉē
eṉṉuṅ kuṟuntokaiyilum,
kaltōyt tuṭutta paṭivap pārppāṉ
eṉṉum mullaip pāṭṭilum `paṭimai `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷantaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkattai uṇarttutal muṉṉarē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉām.ac coṟkaḷ aṅṅaṉam ap poruḷ uṇarttum vaḻiyellāñ caivacamayat tuṟavi kaḷaiyē uṇarttak kāṇṭumaṉṟic, camaṇ tuṟavikaḷai oru ciṟitāyiṉum uṇarttak kāṇkilēm.
mēluñ, camaṇ camayattiṉar muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṉum nampikkaiyuṭaiyarallar; annampikkai yillāmaiyiṉ, muttīvēṭṭu vaḻipaṭum nīrarum. allar; aḵtillāmaiyiṉ, tī niṟattatākiya kāviyāṭaiyum, tīyiṉ eccamākiya tūya veṇṇīṟum mēṟkoḷvārum allar; iṟaivaṉiṉ tiruvaṭaiyāḷamākiya caṭai muṭiyun; taṇṇīrk kuṭuvaiyum, mukkōlum uṭaiyārum allar; ip peṟṟiyiṉarāṉa camaṇ muṉivar `ācīvakarum, `nikaṇṭarum, `muṇṭacāvakarum eṉa muttiṟappaṭuva reṉpatūum, ivaruḷ ācīvakarāvār āṭaiyiṉṟi muṟṟum ammaṇamāyum, nikaṇṭar iṭuppil oruciṟu tuṇṭu maṭṭum uṭuppavarāyum muṇṭa cāvakar araiyil oru tuṇṭu uṭuttut talaiyai maḻittuviṭupavarāyum iruppareṉpatūum aṅkut taranikāyattiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ic camaṇmuṉivarum, ivariṉ vēṟāṉa putta muṉivarkaḷum kautama cākkiyar kālattil iruntāṟpōlavē, talaiyiṟ caṭaimuṭiyuṭaiyaruṅ kaiyil mukkōl ēntiyavarumāṉa pārppaṉat tuṟavikaḷum akkālattil vaṭanāṭṭilum iruntaṉareṉpatūum av aṅkuttara nikāyattilēyē nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.32 eṉavē, kautamacākkiyar irunta kālattilēyē aḵtāvatu iṟṟaikku iraṇṭāyirattain nūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarēyē vaṭanāṭṭiluñ civavaṭivam pūṇṭa pārppaṉa muṉivarkaḷ iruntamai pautta camaya nūlkaḷiṉālēyē pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō? maṟṟut teṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭilō akkālattiṟ pārppaṉa muṉivarait tavira, ēṉaic camaṇ pautta muṉivar iruntilar. ākavē, `paṭimai eṉṉuñ coṟṟeṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta caiva antaṇariṉ tava oḻukkattaiyun tavavaṭivattaiyuñ cuṭṭutaṟkē eḻuntataṉṟic, camaṇ muṉivariṉ tavavoḻukkattai uṇarttutaṟ keḻuntataṉ ṟeṉpatu kaṭaippiṭikka atuvēyumaṉṟi, vaṭanāṭṭilirunta camaṇ muṉivariṉ tavavoḻukkattai uṇarttuñ collāka atu camaṇ camaya nūlkaḷilāyiṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatōveṉa yām an nūlkaḷai ārāyntu pārkka, āṇṭum `paṭimā eṉṉuñ collaik kaṇṭilēm. ac col camaṇ muṉivariṉ tava voḻukkattaiyē kuṟikku meṉa nāṭṭappuk kavarātal, ataṟku oru mēṟkōḷāyiṉuñ camaṇ nūlkaḷilirun teṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉarō veṉṟāl, atutāṉum illai. `paṭimā eṉpatu camaṇ camayac collē eṉpataṟku ētoru mēṟkōḷum paḻaiya camaṇ nūlkaḷilirun teṭuttuk kāṭṭātu, ippōtu cila āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ōr āṅkila mātar eḻutiya ōr āṅkila nūlai nampi accol camaṇ camayattavarkkē yurittātal vēṇṭumeṉat tan tuṇipuraittaliṉumpiḻaipāṭāvatu piṟitillai; ip piḻaipāṭṭuraiyaik koṇṭu āciriyar tolkāppiya ṉāraic camaṇa muṉivareṉa kāṭṭap pukuvatiṉum pētaimaiyāvatum piṟitillai. ārāycci muṟai iṉṉateṉṟaṟiyātār tāmum iṉṉōraṉṉa ārāycciyiṟ pukutal peritum ētamāmeṉka.
ippōtu vaḻaṅkuñ camaṇ nūlkaḷil vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻiyiliruppaṉavellām ki.pi.āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattiliruntu iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa vākumeṉa avai tammai naṉkārāynta airōppiya āciriyarkaḷ kūṟāniṟkiṉṟaṉar.33 ivvāṟu piṟpaṭṭa kālattil eḻutappaṭṭa camaṇnūlkaḷil yāṇṭō arukip `paṭimai eṉṉuñ col camaṇ muṉivariṉ tavavoḻukkattai yuṇarttutaṟku vaḻaṅkappaṭṭirukkalām. atu koṇṭu accol camaṇ camayattavarkkē uritteṉṟal ārāycciyuṇarvillār kūṟṟām. ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇ ṭilirun  teḻutappaṭṭa camaṇ nūlkaṭku ainnūṟāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭa aruntamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉum pāṭṭukaḷiṉu mellām accol antaṇariṉ caiva tavavoḻukkattaiyē uṇarttak kāṇṭalāl, atu caiva camayattavarkkē urittāvataṉṟic camaṇmatattavarkku urittātal oruvāṟṟāṉuñ cellātu. tamakku muṉṉirunta caiva antaṇat tāpatartan tavavoḻukkattukku vaḻaṅki vanta ap `paṭimai yeṉṉuñ collaiyē piṉvanta camaṇ muṉivar tamakkum uriyatāka orōviṭattup payaṉpaṭuttiṉā rākaṟpālār. vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kautama cākkiyar kālattirunta putta camaṇ camayattavaruṅ kūṭat, taṅ kālattiṟku muṉṉirunta caiva antaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkat taiyē mutalāka vaittu, ataṉpaṭi tamakkuriya oḻukalāṟukaḷai varaiyaṟuttamaippā rāyiṉareṉa, ivai tammaip palapaṭiyāṉum naṭuniṉṟu naṉkāynta hermaṉ jākōpi eṉṉum airōppiya āciriyar naṉku viḷakkikkāṭṭi yirukkiṉṟār.34 vaṭanāṭṭiṟ paṇṭaik kālattirunta caiva antaṇat tāpatar civapirāṉ tiruvuruva aṭaiyāḷamāṉa kāviyāṭaiyē uṭuttut talaimēṟ caṭaimuṭiyuṭaiyarā yiruntaṉa reṉpatu, avar tamatu `jaiṉacūttira āṅkila moḻipeyarppiṉ mukavuraiyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya pōtāyaṉa tarumacāttira mēṟkōḷ kaḷāl (2, 6, 11, 15 - 21) naṉku viḷaṅkum. caiva antaṇa muṉivar uṭuttuṅ kāviyāṭaiyaip pārttē, pautta piṭcukkaḷ niṟattāl ataṉai yoppatoru tuvarāṭai yuṭupparāyiṉa reṉpatūum, putta camaṇmuṉivar tuvakkattil vaṭanāṭṭiṉ oru ciṟu pakutiyil iruntamaipōlātu caivamuṉivarar paratanāṭeṅkum paṇṭutoṭṭē paraviyiruntaṉa reṉpatūum piṟavum avarāl naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. uṇmaiyārāycciyiṟ  ṟērnta ivvairōppiya āciriyar eṭuttukkāṭṭiya paṇṭai vaṭanūl mēṟkōḷkaḷaiyum, avaṟṟai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu ārāyntu kāṭṭiya uṇmai muṭipukaḷaiyum naṭuniṉṟu nōkkavallārkkup, putta camaṇmuṉivar tōṉṟutaṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟṟoṭṭē caiva antaṇa aruntavattōr ip parata nāṭeṅkum paraviyiruntamaiyum, piṉvanta putta camaṇ tuṟavōr tamakkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṉṉirunta caiva antaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkattaiyē perumpāṉmaiyun taḻuvi yoḻukalāṉamaiyun teṟṟeṉap pulaṉākā niṟkum. ākavē, tavavoḻukkamum, at tavavoḻukkattai yuṇarttum `paṭimai, `paṭivam `pratimā eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷuñ, caiva antaṇariṉiṉṟu ēṉaip puttacamaṇ camayattārkku vantatākal vēṇṭumēyaṉṟip, piṟkālat tavarāṉa puttar camaṇariliruntu, ivarkkup pallāyiram āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭa caiva muṉivarkkuc ceṉṟatākal ēlātu. ātalāl, muṟkālattatu itu, piṟkālattatu itu eṉṟu pakuttārāyamāṭṭātu, muṉṉatu piṉṉatākavum piṉṉatu muṉṉatākavum piṟaḻakkoṇṭu, paṭimaiyoḻukkañ camaṇarkkē yuriyateṉak karaintāratu urai periyatoru talaitaṭumāṟṟa vuraiyāy muṭintamai kaṇṭu koḷka.
aṟṟēlaḵtāka, tavavoḻukkamum ataṟkēṟṟa tava vaṭivamum ataṉai yuṇarttum `paṭimai eṉṉuñ collum, puttar camaṇar tōṉṟutaṟkup pallāyira āṇṭukaḷ muṉṉarē caiva antaṇarpāl uḷavāyatu uṇmaiyāyiṉ, ataṉai niṟuvutaṟkuriya paḻaiya nūṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷ kāṭṭuka veṉiṉ; avai tammaip paṇṭait tamiḻnūlkaḷiṉiṉṟum muṉṉarē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉām. ap paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ pāṭṭukaḷil yāṇṭum putta camaṇ camayattavar ōreṭṭuṇaiyuṅ kuṟikkappaṭāmaiyum, `paṭimai, `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ caiva antaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkattaiyē kuṟittaṟku avaṟṟuḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭutalum āṇṭē viḷakkip pōntām. ākavē, puttar camaṇar tōṉṟutaṟku mūvāyira āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatākiya tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyira vuraiyiṟ pōnta `paṭimaiyōṉ eṉṉuñcoṟ caiva antaṇa muṉivaṉākiya āciriyaṉ tolkāppiyaṉṟaṉ tavavoḻuk kattaiyun tavavaṭivattaiyum maṭṭumē cuṭṭutal vēṇṭumaṉṟi, avaṟkup peruṅkālam piṟpaṭṭirunta puttar camaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkattaiyātal tavavaṭivattaiyātal cuṭṭutal oru ciṟitum icaiyāteṉ ṟuṇarntukoḷka. tol kāppiyattuḷ yāṇṭum puttacamaṇ camayaṅkaḷ oru tiṉaittaṉaiyuṅ kuṟippiṭap paṭāmaiyum niṉaiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu.
iṉṉuṅ, kautamacākkiyarum makāvīrarun tōṉṟi muṟaiyē puttacamaṇ camayaṅkaḷait tōṟṟuvittaṟku muṉ, cākkiya vakuppiṉar civapirāṉaiyē vaṇaṅki vantaṉa reṉpatu, vaṭanāṭṭiluḷḷa paḻaiya kalveṭṭukaḷiṉ ārāycciyāl naṉku pulaṉākāniṟkiṉṟatu.35 kautama cākkiyar piṟanta ñāṉṟu, avar civapirāṉ tiruvaruḷaip peṟutaṟ poruṭṭuc civapirāṉ tirukkōyiṟkup peṟṟōrāl eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭamai, paḻaiya puttacamayak kalveṭṭu oṉṟiṟ cetukki vaikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.36 mikac ciṟanta ic cāṉṟukaḷai uṟṟārāya vallār evarkkum, putta camaṇmataṅkaḷ tōṉṟutaṟkup pallāyira āṇṭukaḷ muṟṟoṭṭē ip paratanā ṭeṅkaṇum paravi yiruntatu caivacamayamēyā meṉpatūum, iṅku muḻutum ulaviya tāpatar caivacamaya antaṇa muṉivarēyāvareṉpatūun teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
aṟṟēl, `pratimā, `paṭimai `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟēṉum, puttacamaṇ nūlkaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa paḻaiya vaṭanūlkaḷuḷ uṇṭō veṉiṉ; uṇṭu. vaṭanūlkaḷuḷ mikap paḻaiyaṉa irukku, ecur, cāmam eṉpavaikaḷēyām. aiyāyira āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ tamiḻar āriyar eṉṉum iruvakuppiṉarkkum āciriyarāyirunta muṉivarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭuc citarntukiṭanta ivaṟṟiṉ pāṭṭukaḷ pāratap pōr muṭintapiṉ viyācarāl oruṅku tokukkappaṭṭu irukku, ecur, cāmam eṉṉum mūṉṟu vētaṅkaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvētappāṭṭukaḷ vaḻaṅkiya kālattil vaṇaṅkappaṭṭu vanta intiraṉ, varuṇaṉ, uruttiraṉ mutalāṉa teyvaṅkaḷukku uruvaṅkaḷ camaittu vaittu, avaṟṟiṟku vaḻipāṭu āṟṟivantaṉar eṉpatum avai tammaik karuttūṉṟi ārāyumvaḻi iṉitu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. āriyar pōrmēṟ ceṉṟa kālaṅkaḷil intiraṉatu vaṭivattai eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉareṉpatu irukkuvētattiṟ collappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu.37 civapirāṉ tiruvaṭaiyāḷamāṉa civaliṅka vaṭivattaip paṇṭait tamiḻmakkaḷ vaittuvaḻipaṭṭamai irukkuvēta ēḻām maṇṭilattiṉ irupattōrām patikattilum pattām maṇṭilattiṉ toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatām patikattilum viḷakkamāka vaittu nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ivaiyēyaṉṟi, `pratimā eṉṉuñ collē oru teyvampōṟ ceyta vaṭivattaik kuṟikkum moḻiyāka ecurvētattil nataya pratimā ati (32, 3) eṉṟu teḷivākak kūṟappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. iv vuṇmaikaḷaic ciṟitāyiṉum ārāyntu pārkkum aṟivu matukaiyiṉṟi, `pratimā eṉṉuñ col camaṇ nūlkaḷi laṉṟip, paḻaiya vētanūlkaḷil yāṇṭu millai yeṉṟu tuṇinturaittavar tam pētaimaip perukkai eṉṉeṉpēm! iṉiyēṉum accol pautta camaṇ nūlkaḷ tōṉṟutaṟku mūvāyira āṇṭu muṉṉirunta ecurvētattilēyē uḷateṉṟaṟintu avar uṇmai teḷiyakkaṭavārāka.
iṉi, avar,  tolkāppiyaraic camaṇcamayattiṉareṉa nāṭṭutaṟku maṟṟumoru cāṉṟu kāṭṭuvē meṉap pukuntu, uyirkaḷai ōraṟivumutal aiyaṟivu īṟāka uḷḷa aintu vakaikaḷiṟ pakuttaṭakkutal camaṇcamayak kōṭpāṭāku meṉṟun tolkāppiyaṉār ivvaivakai uyirp pākupāṭukaḷait tamatu `marapiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷil eṭuttōtutaliṉ avar camaṇ camayattavarēyātal vēṇṭumeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉār. ituvum oru ciṟitu ārāyaṟpāṟṟu.
camaṇ matattavarkaḷ evvakaippaṭṭa uyiraiyum evvakai ētupaṟṟiyum kollalākātu eṉṉum mikac ciṟanta kollā aṟattaik kaṭaippiṭittu niṉṟavarkaḷ. vaṭanāṭṭiṉkaṇ vantu kuṭiyēṟiya āriyarum avar vaḻic cārnta pārppaṉarum vēḷvi vēṭṭalai ōr ētuvāka vaittukkoṇṭu, eṇṇiṟanta vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷaik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉai neyyiṟ porittu uṇṭuñ, cōmap pūṇṭil iṟakkiya kaḷḷai nirampa aruntiyum veṟiyāṭ ṭayarntu vantamaiyāl, iyaṟkaiyilēyē pulāluṅ kaḷḷum maṟutta caivavēḷāḷa aracar (kṣattiriyar) av vāriyartan tīya oḻukalāṟṟiṉai aruvaruttu, ataṉait taṭaiceytaṟ poruṭṭākavē camaṇ puttakak koḷkaikaḷait tōṟṟuvittu, avaṟṟai eṅkum paravac ceytaṉar. camaṇmata āciyarāṉa `makāvīrarum puttacamaya āciriyarāṉa `kautama cākkiyarum vaṭakkē makatanāṭṭil vaikiya kṣattiriya allatu tamiḻ vēḷāḷa aracamarapiṟ piṟantōrē yāvar. āriyar vaṭanāṭṭiṟ pukuntu āṅkāṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyapōtu, aṅkam, makatam, kāci, kōcalam, vaccī, mallai, cētī, vañcam, kuru, pāñcālam, maccam, cūracēṉam, accakam, avanti, kāntāram, kāmpōcam mutaliya patiṉāṟu nāṭukaḷil nākarikattiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya vēṟu marapiṉarāṉa tamiḻ vēḷāḷarē aracupurintaṉar eṉpatu aṅkuttaranikāyattil (1, 213; 4, 252, 256, 260) cevvaṉē kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. makata nāṭṭilirunta tamiḻar,  muṉṉarē yām viḷakkiyeḻutiya pūrukkaḷaic cērntōrāvar.38 iṅṅaṉamākac camaṇ kōṭpāṭṭaip paravac ceyta makāvīrar tamiḻaraca muṉivarēyākaiyāl, avarkkum akkālattirunta ēṉai vēḷāḷarkkum potuvāka uriya kollā aṟattaiavar valiyuṟuttip paravac ceytatil ētum putumaiyillai. aññāṉṟirunta attamiḻ muṉivar, taṅ kālattiṟkup pallāyira āṇṭu muṉṉirunta āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār mutalāṉa tamiḻc cāṉṟōr kaṇṭuraitta uyirp pākupāṭukaḷaiyē tāmun taḻuvik kūṟiṉārallatu, makāvīrar mutalākap piṉvanta camaṇmuṉivar vakuttataṉaik kaṇṭu tolkāppiyaṉār avai tammaik kūṟiṉārallar.
aḵteṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum: evviṭattiluḷḷa uyiraiyuṅ kollalākā teṉpatē camaṇartaṅ kōṭ pāṭākaiyāl, ak kōṭpāṭṭiṟku icaiyavē uyirkaḷ uyirvāḻum iṭaṅkaḷai avarkaḷ mutalil ārāyn turaippārāyiṉar. uyirkaḷ nilattiluḷḷaṉavum, nīriluḷḷaṉavum, neruppiluḷ ḷaṉavuṅ, kāṟṟiluḷḷaṉavum eṉa nālvakaip paṭumeṉṟu uraittu, āṇṭāṇṭuḷḷa uyirkaḷukku nōyuñ cākkāṭum uṇṭākāvaṇṇam, avaṟṟōṭu iyaintu kāṇappaṭum uyiril poruḷkaḷait tām payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷum muṟaikaḷai viḷakkik kāṭṭutalē camaṇcamaya āciriyar karuttākum. ippōtuḷḷa camaṇ nūlkaḷil mikap paḻaiyatākiya ācāraṅkacūttiram, curutakantam, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, ēḻām attiyāyaṅkaḷil, uyirkaḷ ulavutaṟkiṭamāṉa nilaṉnīr neruppu kāṟṟu eṉṉum iṭavakaiyāl avvuyirkaḷai nālvakaippaṭuttu, avai tamakku iṭar nērāvaṇṇam aṅkaṅkuḷḷa ēṉaip poruḷkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttumāṟu viritturaittal kāṇka; itaṉ āṟām attiyayaṉattil, īraṟivu mūvaṟivu nālaṟivu aiyaṟivu uṭaiya ellā uyirkaḷukkum iṉpa tuṉpamum accamum uḷavākaliṉ, avai tamakku enta ētuvaik koṇṭum evvakai iṭaruñ ceytalākā teṉṉum attuṇaiyē vaṟpuṟutturaikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟa tallāmal, ōraṟivu mutal aiyaṟivu īṟāka vaittu uyirkaḷaip pākupāṭu ceytal āṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭavillai. ākavē, uyirkaḷai iṭavakaiyāl nālvakaippaṭa vakuttuk kāṭṭutalē paṇṭaic camaṇ āciriyartaṅ karuttātal kāṇṭumaṉṟi, aṟivuvakaiyāṟ pakuttukkāṭṭutal avarkkuk karuttātal kaṇṭilam. iṭavakaiyāṟ pakuttalēavartaṅ kōṭpāṭṭukkup payaṉpaṭuvataṉṟi, aṟivu vakaiyāṟ pakuttal ataṟkuc ciṟitum payaṉpaṭuvataṉṟām; eṉṉai? īraṟivuṭaiya vāyiṉum allatu aiyaṟivuṭaiyavāyiṉum ellāvuyirkkum iṉpatuṉpamum accamum uṭaiyavākaiyāl, aṟiviṉ ēṟṟattāḻvu karutātu ellāvuyirkaḷaiyum pātukāttalē ceyaṟpāla teṉpatūum, uyirkaḷ ōriṭattum uḷa ōriṭattum ila eṉa aṟiyāmaiyāṟ karutiviṭātu nilaṉ nīr neruppu kāṟṟu eṉṉum nāṉkiṭaṅkaḷilum avai uḷavātalaik karuttūṉṟi yārāyntu pārttal iṉṟiyamaiyātateṉpatūum avartaṅ karuttākali ṉeṉka. eṉavē, uyirkaḷai iṭavakaiyāl ārāytalē paṇṭaic camaṇāciriyar kōṭpāṭākumallatu aṟivu vakaiyāl ārāytal avartam kōṭpāṭākāmai peṟṟām. peṟavē, uyirkaḷai iṭavakaiyāṟ pakuttu virittuk kāṭṭiya paḻaiya ācāraṅka cūttirattiṟku māṟāka, avai tammai iṭavakaiyālum aṟivu vakaiyālum pakuttu viritturaitta uttirāttiyayaṉanūl (34) mikavum piṟpaṭṭa kālatteḻunta camaṇmuṉivarāl tolkāppiyattaip pārttuc ceyyappaṭṭa toṉṟāka vēṇṭumākaliṉ, aḵtu īṇṭaikku mēṟkōḷākāteṉa maṟukka. ācāraṅka cūttirattiṟkuc cīlāṅka muṉivar eḻutiyavarai ki.pi. 876 il muṭikkappaṭṭa toṉṟākaiyāl,39 ācāraṅka cūttirattiṉ kālaṅ ki.pi. aintu allatu āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭatākātu. `uttarāttiyayaṉam camaṇ nūlkaḷil mikap piṟpaṭṭatātalum, ataṉ iṟutik kaṇ uyirkaḷai iṭavakaiyālum aṟivuvakaiyālum pākupāṭu ceytu virittōtum muppattāṟām attiyayaṉam iṉṉum piṟpaṭṭa kālattē ceytu cērkkappaṭṭatātalum mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa airōppiya āciriyarāl naṉku viḷakkik kāṭṭap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa40 paḻaiyatākiya `ācāraṅka cūttiram mutaṟkaṇ uyirkaḷai iṭavakaiyāṟ pakuttu virittōti aṟivuvakaiyāṟ pakuttalai oru coṟṟo ṭaraḷavil mikac curukkik kūṟāniṟka, `uttarāttiyayaṉamō iṭavakaiyāṉ maṭṭumēyaṉṟi aṟivu vakaiyālum uyirkaḷaip pakuttu mika virittōtā niṟkiṉṟatu; 20.	It was bot so much an invasion as an advance, and we can easily imagine that it must have an achievement of no small difficulty for a body of men necessarily very inferior in numbers, in the face of compact population, brave, stubborn, and strongly organized. Such the Dravidians are now, when the number, over twentyeight millions south of the Vindhya, and there is not the slightest reason to doubt that such in the main, they were at the early time of their long patriotic struggle Vedic India, p. 295.
21.	It will be surprise to many that the Aryan population of the Indian continent should be so out of all proportion small when compared to the descendants and representatives of those races which the Aryan immigrants found in possion. The same difference must have existed on a still greater scale in those earliest times - and would alonse suffiee to stamp as irrational the theory of Aryan supremacy having been established by sheer conquest and force” Ibid,p.314.
22.	To keep strictly within the information supplied by the Rig-Veda itself - Vasishtha was the bard of the Tritsu the leading and purest Aryan tribe, and Visvamitra was the bard of the Bharatas, their great enemies and one of the most powerful native tribes. He at one time had been with the Tritsu, and for whatever cause he left them -not improbably personal revenge - he played a conspicuous part in the confederacy which attempted to check the Aryan advance and increasing power. There is a hymn (53), in Book III, that of the Visvamitra family, which evidently alludes to this very thing. In the first part of the hymn it is said that when visvamitra conducted kings sudas sacrifies, Indra was gracious to him for the Rishi’s sake, and a great blessing is pronounced on the king, and his war -steed and the expedition on which he starts,. Then , quite suddenly, visvamitry is made to declare in his own person, that his prayers protect of imprecation against enemies who are not named, but whom tradition so positively inentified with vasishtha and his family that the priests of this house in later times never uttered these four verses, and tried not to hearthem when spoken by other Brahmans. It is most probable that the Visavamitras resented some distinction conferred upon the vasishthas, possibly their appointment as purohitas to the Tritsu royal family and went over to theri most powerful enemies, the Purus andharatas. The Tritsu and their allies were veictorious in the ensuingstruggle known as “the war of the ten kings” and both the bards have lfet descriptions of it and of the final battle on the Parushni, in some spirited hymns, the most undoubtedly historical of the collection. At a later period the followers of  Vasishtha and his descendants represent the narrowl orthodox Brahminic school, with its petty punctillousness in the matter of forms, rites observances, its intolerance in the matter of forms, rites, observances, its on tolerance of everthing in -Aryan, its rigid separatism. This school it was which stood guard through all these ages. and up to our day the champion -and possibly orginally the institutor of case who advanced and upheld all the exaggerated claims of the Brahman priesthoo, to divinity. To the rule of the world and ownership of all it holds, to supernatural compelling powers over nature and the gods themselves through sacrifice and ascetic practices. and the likd. The followers of visvamitra nd his descendants, on the other hand, represented the school of liberalism and progress of conciliation and amalgamation. Vedic Indian, pp. 318 - 320.

The Vishnin bracketed with the Siva, which is thought to be a name of the Tugra, one of the oldest aboriginal Dravidian peoples, whom the Aryas had specially nick named 	 ‘Sons of the Serpent’ and who under the religious designation of Siva, were very probably the originators of the worship of siva under the form or with the attribute of a snake:” Ibid, p.328.
